* `Pro 10:15` The rich *_man_*'s wealth is his strong city. The destruction of the poor is their poverty.
* `Pro 10:23` To do mischief is pleasure to a fool, but a *_man_* of understanding has wisdom.

* `Pro 12:3` A *_man_* shall not be established by wickedness, but the root of the righteous shall not be moved.
* `Pro 12:8` A *_man_* shall be commended according to his wisdom, but he who is of a perverse heart shall be despised.
* `Pro 12:10` A righteous *_man_* regards the life of his beast, but the tender mercies of the wicked are cruel.
* `Pro 12:14` A *_man_* shall be satisfied with good by the fruit of his mouth, and the recompense of a *_man_*'s hands shall be rendered to him.
* `Pro 12:16` A fool's wrath is presently known, but a prudent *_man_* covers shame.
* `Pro 12:23` A prudent *_man_* conceals knowledge, but the heart of fools proclaims foolishness.
* `Pro 12:25` Heaviness in the heart of *_man_* depresses it, but a good word makes it glad.
* `Pro 12:27` The slothful *_man_* does not roast that which he took in hunting, but the substance of a diligent *_man_* is precious.
* `Pro 13:2` A *_man_* shall eat good by the fruit of his mouth, but the soul of the transgressors shall eat violence.
* `Pro 13:5` A righteous *_man_* hates lying, but a wicked *_man_* is loathsome and comes to shame.
* `Pro 13:8` The ransom of a *_man_*'s life is his riches, but the poor does not hear rebuke.
* `Pro 13:16` Every prudent *_man_* deals with knowledge, but a fool lays open his folly.
* `Pro 13:22` A good *_man_* leaves an inheritance to his children's children, and the wealth of the sinner is laid up for the just.
* `Pro 14:7` Go from the presence of a foolish *_man_*, when you do not perceive in him the lips of knowledge.
* `Pro 14:12` There is a way which seems right to a *_man_*, but the ways of death are at its end.
* `Pro 14:14` The backslider in heart shall be filled from his own ways, and a good *_man_* from himself.
* `Pro 14:15` The simple believes every word, but the prudent *_man_* looks well to his going.
* `Pro 14:16` A wise *_man_* fears and departs from evil, but the fool rages and is confident.
* `Pro 14:17` He who is quick to anger deals foolishly, and a *_man_* of wicked devices is hated.
* `Pro 15:18` A wrathful *_man_* stirs up strife, but he who is slow to anger appeases strife.
* `Pro 15:19` The way of the slothful *_man_* is like a hedge of thorns, but the way of the righteous is made plain.
* `Pro 15:20` A wise son makes a glad father, but a foolish *_man_* despises his mother.
* `Pro 15:21` Folly is joy to him who is destitute of wisdom, but a *_man_* of understanding walks uprightly.
* `Pro 15:23` A *_man_* has joy by the answer of his mouth, and a word spoken in due season, how good it is!
* `Pro 16:1` The preparations of the heart are in *_man_*, but the answer of the tongue is from the LORD.
* `Pro 16:2` All the ways of a *_man_* are clean in his own eyes, but the LORD weighs the spirits.
* `Pro 16:7` When a *_man_*'s ways please the LORD, he makes even his enemies to be at peace with him.
* `Pro 16:9` A *_man_*'s heart devises his way, but the LORD directs his steps.
* `Pro 16:14` The wrath of a king is as messengers of death, but a wise *_man_* will pacify it.
* `Pro 16:25` There is a way that seems right to a *_man_*, but the ways of death are at its end.
* `Pro 16:27` An ungodly *_man_* digs up evil, and his speech is like a burning fire.
* `Pro 16:28` A perverse *_man_* sows strife, and a whisperer separates chief friends.
* `Pro 16:29` A violent *_man_* entices his neighbor and leads him into the way that is not good.
* `Pro 17:10` A reproof enters more into a wise *_man_* than one hundred stripes into a fool.
* `Pro 17:11` An evil *_man_* seeks only rebellion; therefore, a cruel messenger shall be sent against him.

* `Pro 17:18` A *_man_* void of understanding strikes hands and becomes surety in the presence of his friend.
* `Pro 17:23` A wicked *_man_* takes a gift out of the bosom to pervert the ways of judgment.
* `Pro 17:27` He who has knowledge spares his words, and a *_man_* of understanding is of an excellent spirit.
* `Pro 17:28` Even a fool, when he holds his peace, is counted wise, and he who shuts his lips is esteemed a *_man_* of understanding.
* `Pro 18:1` Through desire a *_man_*, having separated himself, seeks and intermeddles with all wisdom.
* `Pro 18:4` The words of a *_man_*'s mouth are as deep waters, and the well-spring of wisdom as a flowing brook.
* `Pro 18:11` The rich *_man_*'s wealth is his strong city, and like a high wall in his own conceit.
* `Pro 18:12` Before destruction the heart of *_man_* is haughty, and before honor is humility.
* `Pro 18:14` The spirit of a *_man_* will sustain his infirmity, but who can bear a wounded spirit?
* `Pro 18:16` A *_man_*'s gift makes room for him, and brings him before great men.
* `Pro 18:20` A *_man_*'s belly shall be satisfied with the fruit of his mouth. And with the increase of his lips he shall be filled.
* `Pro 18:24` A *_man_* of many friends may come to ruin, but there is a friend who sticks closer than a brother.
* `Pro 19:3` The foolishness of *_man_* perverts his way, and his heart frets against the LORD.
* `Pro 19:6` Many will entreat the favor of the prince. And every *_man_* is a friend to him who gives gifts.
* `Pro 19:11` The discretion of a *_man_* defers his anger, and it is his glory to pass over a transgression.
* `Pro 19:19` A *_man_* of great wrath shall suffer punishment, for if you deliver him, yet you must do it again.
* `Pro 19:21` There are many devices in a *_man_*'s heart, but the counsel of the LORD, that shall stand.
* `Pro 19:22` The desire of a *_man_* is his kindness. And a poor *_man_* is better than a liar.
* `Pro 19:24` A slothful *_man_* hides his hand in his bosom and will not so much as bring it to his mouth again.
* `Pro 20:3` It is an honor for a *_man_* to cease from strife, but every fool will be meddling.
* `Pro 20:5` Counsel in the heart of *_man_* is like deep water, but a *_man_* of understanding will draw it out.
* `Pro 20:6` Most men will each proclaim his own goodness, but who can find a faithful *_man_*?
* `Pro 20:7` The just *_man_* walks in his integrity. His children are blessed after him.
* `Pro 20:17` Bread of deceit is sweet to a *_man_*, but afterward his mouth shall be filled with gravel.
* `Pro 20:24` *_Man_*'s goings are of the LORD. How can a *_man_* then understand his own way?
* `Pro 20:25` It is a snare to the *_man_* who devours that which is holy and later vows to make inquiry.
* `Pro 20:27` The spirit of *_man_* is the candle of the LORD, searching all the inward parts of the belly.
* `Pro 21:2` Every way of a *_man_* is right in his own eyes, but the LORD ponders the hearts.
* `Pro 21:8` Crooked is the way of a guilty *_man_*, but as for the pure, his work is right.
* `Pro 21:12` The righteous *_man_* wisely considers the house of the wicked, but God overthrows the wicked for their wickedness.
* `Pro 21:16` The *_man_* who wanders out of the way of understanding shall remain in the congregation of the dead.
* `Pro 21:17` He who loves pleasure shall be a poor *_man_*. He who loves wine and oil shall not be rich.
* `Pro 21:20` There is a treasure to be desired, and oil in the dwelling of the wise, but a foolish *_man_* spends it up.
* `Pro 21:22` A wise *_man_* scales the city of the mighty and casts down the strength of its confidence.
* `Pro 21:24` The proud and haughty *_man_*, "Scoffer," is his name. He works in the arrogance of pride.
* `Pro 21:28` A false witness shall perish, but the *_man_* who listens shall speak forever.
* `Pro 21:29` A wicked *_man_* hardens his face, but as for the upright, he directs his way.
* `Pro 22:3` A prudent *_man_* foresees the evil and hides himself, but the simple pass on and are punished.

* `Pro 22:24` Make no friendship with an angry *_man_*, and with a furious *_man_* you shall not go,
* `Pro 22:29` Do you see a *_man_* diligent in his business? He shall stand before kings. He shall not stand before obscure men.
* `Pro 23:2` And put a knife to your throat, if you are a *_man_* given to appetite.
* `Pro 23:21` For the drunkard and the glutton shall come to poverty, and drowsiness shall clothe a *_man_* with rags.
* `Pro 24:5` A wise *_man_* is strong, and a *_man_* of knowledge increases strength.
* `Pro 24:12` If you say, "Behold, we did not know it," does he who ponders the heart not consider it? And he who keeps your soul, does he not know it? And will he not render to every *_man_* according to his works?

* `Pro 24:34` And your poverty shall come as one who travels, and your need as an armed *_man_*.
* `Pro 25:18` A *_man_* who bears false witness against his neighbor is a maul, a sword, and a sharp arrow.
* `Pro 25:19` Confidence in an unfaithful *_man_* in time of trouble is like a broken tooth and a foot out of joint.
* `Pro 25:26` A righteous *_man_* falling down before the wicked is as a turbid fountain and a corrupt spring.
* `Pro 26:12` Do you see a *_man_* wise in his own conceit? There is more hope for a fool than for him.
* `Pro 26:13` The slothful *_man_* says, "There is a lion in the way. A lion is in the streets."
* `Pro 26:18` As a mad *_man_* who casts fire-brands, arrows, and death,
* `Pro 26:19` So is the *_man_* who deceives his neighbor, and says, "Am I not joking?"
* `Pro 26:21` As coals are to burning coals and wood to fire, so is a contentious *_man_* to kindle strife.
* `Pro 27:2` Let another *_man_* praise you, and not your own mouth, a stranger, and not your own lips.
* `Pro 27:8` As a bird that wanders from her nest, so is a *_man_* who wanders from his place.
* `Pro 27:9` Ointment and perfume rejoice the heart, and so does the sweetness of a *_man_*'s friend by hearty counsel.
* `Pro 27:12` A prudent *_man_* foresees the evil and hides himself, but the simple pass on and are punished.
* `Pro 27:17` As iron sharpens iron, so a *_man_* sharpens the countenance of his friend.
* `Pro 27:19` As in water face reflects face, so the heart of *_man_* to *_man_*.
* `Pro 27:20` Hell and destruction are never full, and the eyes of *_man_* are never satisfied.
* `Pro 27:21` As the refining-pot for silver and the furnace for gold, so is a *_man_* to his praise.
* `Pro 28:1` The wicked flee when no *_man_* pursues, but the righteous are as bold as a lion.
* `Pro 28:2` For the transgression of a land many are its princes, but by a *_man_* of understanding and knowledge the state thereof shall be prolonged.
* `Pro 28:3` A poor *_man_* who oppresses the poor is like a sweeping rain which leaves no food.
* `Pro 28:11` The rich *_man_* is wise in his own conceit, but the poor *_man_* who has understanding searches him out.
* `Pro 28:12` When righteous men rejoice, there is great glory, but when the wicked rise, a *_man_* is hidden.
* `Pro 28:14` Happy is the *_man_* who fears always, but he who hardens his heart shall fall into mischief.
* `Pro 28:17` A *_man_* who does violence to the blood of any person shall flee to the pit. Let no *_man_* stay him.
* `Pro 28:20` A faithful *_man_* shall abound with blessings, but he who makes haste to be rich shall not be innocent.
* `Pro 28:21` To have respect of persons is not good, because for a piece of bread a *_man_* will transgress.
* `Pro 28:23` He who rebukes a *_man_* afterward shall find more favor than he who flatters with the tongue.
* `Pro 29:5` A *_man_* who flatters his neighbor spreads a net for his feet.
* `Pro 29:6` In the transgression of an evil *_man_* there is a snare, but the righteous sings and rejoices.
* `Pro 29:9` If a wise *_man_* contends with a foolish *_man_*, whether he rages or laughs, there is no rest.
* `Pro 29:11` A fool utters all his mind, but a wise *_man_* keeps it in until afterward.
* `Pro 29:13` The poor and the deceitful *_man_* meet together, the LORD enlightens both their eyes.
* `Pro 29:20` Do you see a *_man_* who is hasty in his words? There is more hope for a fool than for him.
* `Pro 29:22` An angry *_man_* stirs up strife, and a furious *_man_* abounds in transgression.
* `Pro 29:23` A *_man_*'s pride shall bring him low, but honor shall uphold the humble in spirit.
* `Pro 29:25` The fear of *_man_* brings a snare, but he who puts his trust in the LORD shall be safe.
* `Pro 29:26` Many seek the ruler's favor, but every *_man_*'s judgment comes from the LORD.
* `Pro 29:27` An unjust *_man_* is an abomination to the just, and he who is upright in the way is an abomination to the wicked.
* `Pro 30:1` [The words of Agur the son of Jakeh, even the prophecy. The *_man_* spoke to Ithiel, even to Ithiel and Ucal.]
* `Pro 30:2` Surely I am more brutish than any *_man_* and do not have the understanding of a *_man_*.
* `Pro 30:19` The way of an eagle in the air, the way of a serpent upon a rock, the way of a ship in the midst of the sea, and the way of a *_man_* with a young woman.
* `Ecc 1:3` What profit does a *_man_* have of all his labor, which he takes under the sun?
* `Ecc 1:8` All things are full of labor. *_Man_* cannot utter it. The eye is not satisfied with seeing, nor the ear filled with hearing.
* `Ecc 1:13` And I gave my heart to seek and search out by wisdom concerning all things that are done under heaven. This is a grievous labor that God has given to the sons of *_man_* to be exercised with.
* `Ecc 2:12` And I turned myself to behold wisdom, madness, and folly. For what can the *_man_* do who comes after the king? Even that which has already been done.
* `Ecc 2:14` The wise *_man_*'s eyes are in his head, but the fool walks in darkness. And I myself perceived also that one event happens to them all.
* `Ecc 2:16` For there is no remembrance of the wise more than of the fool forever, seeing that which now is in the days to come shall all be forgotten. And how does the wise *_man_* die? As the fool.
* `Ecc 2:18` Indeed, I hated all my labor which I had taken under the sun, because I must leave it to the *_man_* who shall be after me.
* `Ecc 2:19` And who knows whether he shall be a wise *_man_* or a fool? Yet he shall have rule over all my labor in which I have labored and in which I have shown myself wise under the sun. This is also vanity.
* `Ecc 2:21` For there is a *_man_* whose labor is in wisdom, in knowledge, and in equity, yet to a *_man_* who has not labored in it, he shall leave it for his portion. This also is vanity and a great evil.
* `Ecc 2:22` For what does *_man_* have of all his labor and of the vexation of his heart in which he has labored under the sun?
* `Ecc 2:24` There is nothing better for a *_man_* than that he should eat and drink, and that he should make his soul enjoy good in his labor. This also I saw, that it was from the hand of God.
* `Ecc 2:26` For to the *_man_* who is good in his sight, God gives wisdom, knowledge, and joy, but to the sinner he gives toil, to gather and to amass, so that he may give to him who is good before God. This also is vanity and vexation of spirit.
* `Ecc 3:11` He has made everything beautiful in its time. He also has set the world in their heart so that no *_man_* can find out the work that God makes from the beginning to the end.
* `Ecc 3:12` I know that there is no good in them, but for a *_man_* to rejoice and to do good in his life.
* `Ecc 3:13` And also that every *_man_* should eat and drink and enjoy the good of all his labor, is the gift of God.
* `Ecc 3:19` For that which befalls the sons of men befalls beasts, even one thing befalls them: as one dies, so the other dies. Indeed, they all have one breath, so that a *_man_* has no pre-eminence above a beast, for all is vanity.
* `Ecc 3:21` Who knows whether the spirit of *_man_* goes upward, or whether the spirit of the beast goes downward to the earth?
* `Ecc 3:22` Therefore, I perceive that there is nothing better than that a *_man_* should rejoice in his own works, for that is his portion. For who shall bring him to see what shall be after him?
* `Ecc 4:4` Again, I considered all labor and every right work, that for this, a *_man_* is envied by his neighbor. This is also vanity and vexation of spirit.
* `Ecc 5:12` The sleep of a laboring *_man_* is sweet, whether he eats little or much, but the abundance of the rich will not allow him to sleep.
* `Ecc 5:19` Every *_man_* also to whom God has given riches and wealth and has given him power to eat of it, to take his portion, and to rejoice in his labor—this is the gift of God.
* `Ecc 6:2` A *_man_* to whom God has given riches, wealth, and honor, so that he lacks nothing for his soul of all that he desires, yet God does not give him power to enjoy them, but a stranger enjoys them. This is vanity, and it is an evil disease.
* `Ecc 6:3` If a *_man_* begets a hundred children and lives many years, so that the days of his years are many, but his soul is not filled with good and he also has no burial, I say that the one of an untimely birth is better than he is.
* `Ecc 6:7` All the labor of *_man_* is for his mouth, and yet the appetite is not filled.
* `Ecc 6:10` That which has been is named already, and it is known what *_man_* is, for he cannot contend with him who is mightier than he is.
* `Ecc 6:11` Seeing there are many things that increase vanity, what is *_man_* the better?
* `Ecc 6:12` For who knows what is good for *_man_* in this life, all the days of his vain life which he spends as a shadow? For who can tell a *_man_* what shall be after him under the sun?
* `Ecc 7:5` It is better to hear the rebuke of the wise, than for a *_man_* to hear the song of fools.
* `Ecc 7:7` Surely oppression makes a wise *_man_* mad, and a gift destroys the heart.
* `Ecc 7:14` In the day of prosperity be joyful, but in the day of adversity consider this: God also has set one over against the other, to the end that *_man_* should find nothing after him.
* `Ecc 7:15` I have seen all things in the days of my vanity. There is a just *_man_* who perishes in his righteousness, and there is a wicked *_man_* who prolongs his life in his wickedness.
* `Ecc 7:20` For there is not a just *_man_* upon earth who does good and does not sin.
* `Ecc 7:28` Which yet my soul seeks, but I do not find. One *_man_* among a thousand I have found, but a woman among all those I have not found.
* `Ecc 7:29` Behold, I have only found this: that God has made *_man_* upright, but they have sought out many inventions."
* `Ecc 8:1` Who is like the wise *_man_*? And who knows the interpretation of a thing? A *_man_*'s wisdom causes his face to shine, and the boldness of his face shall be changed.
* `Ecc 8:5` He who keeps the commandment shall feel no evil thing. And a wise *_man_*'s heart discerns both time and judgment.
* `Ecc 8:6` For to every purpose there is time and judgment; therefore, the misery of *_man_* is great upon him.
* `Ecc 8:8` There is no *_man_* who has power over the spirit to retain the spirit, neither does he have power in the day of death. And there is no discharge in that war, neither shall wickedness deliver those who are given to it.
* `Ecc 8:9` All this I have seen, applying my heart to every work that is done under the sun. There is a time in which one *_man_* rules over another to his own hurt.
* `Ecc 8:15` Then I commended mirth, because a *_man_* has no better thing under the sun than to eat, to drink, and to be merry, for that shall abide with him in his labor all the days of his life which God gives him under the sun.

* `Phl 4:23` *_May_* the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all. Amen.

* `Col 4:6` Let your speech always be with grace, seasoned with salt, so that you *_may_* know how you ought to answer every man.

* `1Th 3:10` Night and day praying exceedingly that we *_may_* see your face and *_may_* perfect that which is lacking in your faith?

* `1Th 3:12` And *_may_* the Lord make you to increase and abound in love toward one another and toward all men, even as we do toward you,

* `1Th 4:12` So that you *_may_* walk honestly toward those who are outside and that you *_may_* have need of nothing.
* `1Th 5:23` And *_may_* the very God of peace sanctify you wholly. And *_may_* your whole spirit, soul, and body be preserved blameless to the coming of our Lord Jesus Christ.
* `1Th 5:28` *_May_* the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you. Amen.

* `2Th 2:6` And now you know that which restrains him, to the end that he *_may_* be revealed in his own season.
* `2Th 2:12` So that they all *_may_* be condemned who did not believe the truth, but had pleasure in unrighteousness.
* `2Th 2:16` Now *_may_* our Lord Jesus Christ himself and God, even our Father, who has loved us and has given us everlasting consolation and good hope through grace,

* `2Th 3:2` And that we *_may_* be delivered from unreasonable and wicked men, for all men do not have faith.
* `2Th 3:5` And *_may_* the Lord direct your hearts into the love of God and into the patient waiting for Christ.
* `2Th 3:14` And if any man does not obey our word by this epistle, note that man and have no company with him so that he *_may_* be ashamed.
* `2Th 3:16` Now *_may_* the Lord of peace himself give you peace always by all means. *_May_* the Lord be with you all.
* `2Th 3:18` *_May_* the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all. Amen.
* `1Ti 1:18` This charge I commit to you, son Timothy, according to the prophecies which went before to you, so that by them you *_may_* war a good warfare,
* `1Ti 1:20` Of whom are Hymeneus and Alexander, whom I have delivered to Satan so that they *_may_* learn not to blaspheme.
* `1Ti 2:2` For kings, and for all who are in authority, so that we *_may_* lead a quiet and peaceable life in all godliness and honesty.

* `1Ti 4:15` Meditate upon these things. Give yourself wholly to them so that your profiting *_may_* appear to all.
* `1Ti 5:7` And these things give in charge, so that they *_may_* be blameless.

* `1Ti 5:20` Rebuke those who sin in the sight of all so that others *_may_* also fear.
* `1Ti 6:1` Let as many servants as are under the yoke count their own masters worthy of all honor so that the name of God and his doctrine *_may_* not be blasphemed.
* `1Ti 6:19` Laying up in store for themselves a good foundation against the time to come, so that they *_may_* take hold of eternal life.
* `2Ti 1:4` Greatly desiring to see you, being mindful of your tears, so that I *_may_* be filled with joy,
* `2Ti 1:16` *_May_* the Lord give mercy to the house of Onesiphorus, for he often refreshed me and was not ashamed of my bonds.
* `2Ti 1:18` *_May_* the Lord grant to him that he *_may_* find mercy from the Lord in that day. And in all that he ministered to me at Ephesus, you know very well.
* `2Ti 2:4` No man who wars entangles himself with the affairs of this life so that he *_may_* please him who has chosen him to be a soldier.
* `2Ti 2:7` Consider what I say, and *_may_* the Lord give you understanding in all things.
* `2Ti 2:10` Therefore, I endure all things for the sake of the elect so that they *_may_* also obtain the salvation which is in Christ Jesus with eternal glory.
* `2Ti 2:26` And that they *_may_* recover themselves out of the snare of the devil, who are taken captive by him at his will.
* `2Ti 3:17` So that the man of God *_may_* be complete, thoroughly furnished to all good works.
* `2Ti 4:14` Alexander the copper-smith did me much evil—*_may_* the Lord reward him according to his works—
